This is numerical discovery, not a verified theorem.\nNo fact submission, solver, benchmark DP, installation, or extra word.\n\n## Fixed mathematical object and direct derivation\n\nFor enclosing m in {2,6,10,14}, put lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-1/m,\nc=lambda(1+1/m), a=beta/c. Define H_0 empty and\nH_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), j=0,...,m-1, keeping this SAME m throughout.\nThus n=2^m-1. Equivalently physical h_i=1+a*c^v2(i+1), 0<=i<n,\nwhere v2(k) is the exponent of 2 dividing the positive integer k.\nThe equivalence follows by splitting the sequence at its central odd bridge:\nleft and right halves repeat H_(m-1), and the central index has v2=m-1.\n\nPut r_tree=2/c, Zgeom=sum_(j<m)r_tree^j, w_j=r_tree^j/Zgeom. The full\nspace has an orthonormal coordinate for every internal binary-tree node\nv of depth less than m. For each leaf s in {0,1}^m, the feature a_s has\ncoordinate sqrt(w_j)*(-1)^s_(j+1) at its depth-j ancestor and zero\nelsewhere. Define F(x)=log(2^-m sum_s exp(<a_s,x>)).\n\nThe feature mean is zero: at each coordinate the leaves in its left and\nright halves cancel. Distinct coordinate rows are orthogonal, since they\nhave disjoint support or the descendant's signs cancel inside an ancestor's\nconstant-sign support. Each row has positive norm. Therefore the feature\nmatrix has full row rank. Jensen gives F>=0, with equality precisely when\nall leaf logits coincide; their mean is zero, and full row rank then forces\nx=0. Thus the full minimizer is uniquely 0 and F_star=0.\n\nDifferentiation of the finite sum gives gradient E_p a and Hessian\nCov_p(a). For a real random variable in [u,v], its variance is at most\n(v-u)^2/4: its second moment about the midpoint is at most that number,\nand variance is the minimum second moment about a constant. Applying this\nto every unit projection bounds the Hessian by one quarter of the squared\nfeature diameter. Leaves first diverging at depth j have squared distance\n4w_j+2 sum_(k>j)w_k. The corresponding quarter distances decrease with j,\nbecause w_j-w_(j+1)/2>0. Their maximum is (1+w_0)/2, denoted Ltree.\nThis is a globally valid gradient Lipschitz constant. It is also sharp:\nthe full row rank maps onto all zero-mean leaf logits, allowing probability\nto approach equal mass on any selected pair of root-diverging leaves.\nThe covariance in their difference direction approaches Ltree.\n\nInitialize only the left-spine coordinates x_(0^j)=log(2)/sqrt(w_j);\nall other coordinates are zero. The TRUE initial squared radius is\nR2=(log 2)^2 sum_j 1/w_j=(log 2)^2 r_tree^(1-m) Zgeom^2.\nThe second equality is the finite geometric-sum reversal identity.\n\nA first-right-turn group at depth k<m contains 2^(m-k-1) leaves;\nthe final all-left group k=m contains one leaf. Its uniform reference\nmass is q_k=2^(-k-1) for k<m and q_m=2^-m. If y_j=sqrt(w_j)x_(0^j),\ntheir logits are ell_k=sum_(j<k)y_j-y_k for k<m and ell_m=sum_j y_j.\nAll nodes off the spine lie inside one group. When their coordinates\nvanish, its leaves have equal logits and equal probabilities; hence its\ntwo descendant halves give zero gradient on that coordinate. Induction\nproves these coordinates remain zero at EVERY physical query.\n\nConsequently P_k=q_k exp(ell_k)/sum_l q_l exp(ell_l), and the actual\nspine gradient has coordinate sqrt(w_j)d_j, where\nd_j=sum_(k>j)P_k-P_j. The complete actual step is\ny'_j=y_j-(h_i*w_j/Ltree)d_j. No child restart or transition labels occur.\nInitially q_k exp(ell_k)=1/4 for k<m and 1 for k=m; thus\nP_k=1/(m+4), P_m=4/(m+4), F(x0)=log((m+4)/4).\nAlso sum_k q_k ell_k=0, so F=sum_k q_k log(q_k/P_k).\n\n## Stable evaluation of this identical finite function\n\nLet G_m=0 be the log partition of the final empty subtree. Splitting the\ndepth-j subtree into its right zero-field group and left spine gives\nG_j=log((exp(-y_j)+exp(y_j+G_(j+1)))/2)\n   =G_(j+1)/2+logcosh(t_j), t_j=y_j+G_(j+1)/2.\nThus F=G_0, with every term nonnegative. Evaluate logcosh(t) as\nlog1p(2*sinh(t/2)^2) for |t|<=20, and as\n|t|+log1p(exp(-2|t|))-log(2) otherwise. This avoids the cancellation\nof subtracting log(2) or a shifted normalizer near the minimizer.\n\nThe conditional left probability at node j is sigmoid(2t_j), the right\nprobability sigmoid(-2t_j). Starting at reach mass U_0=1, set\nP_j=U_j sigmoid(-2t_j), U_(j+1)=U_j sigmoid(2t_j), and P_m=U_m.\nBoth sigmoid signs use exp(-2|t_j|), never a positive large exponent.\nThe exact derivative difference is d_j=U_j*tanh(t_j), evaluated in that\nform to avoid cancellation between almost equal probabilities.\nThis is an algebraic evaluation of the SAME P, not a new update rule.\n\nThe binary64 jobs also evaluate the grouped shifted-log-sum-exp objective\nas a diagnostic, retaining its difference from G_0. They retain every y,\nP, and actual objective at every index 0,...,n. Every residual is saved even if it\npasses. Calibration requires finite states, all equality/sector/factor checks,\nand all n physical updates in BOTH implementations. A nonfinite state or\noverflow invalidates the case and stops it; never clip, rescale, project, change\nstep, repair the mean, or resume it. Finite calibration residual failures finish\nthe original m8 trajectory for diagnosis but prevent discovery authorization.\nDiscovery first-child/central diagnostics are also guarded; failed cases are\nlabeled invalid and cannot supply a terminal observation. Cancellation means |a-b|<=64eps(|a|+|b|)\nfor nonzero neighboring operands; it is an indicator, not proof of lost accuracy.\nNumPy flags do not certify the absence of libm or Python-scalar underflow.\nDo not delete ties; max(y,0) is the function definition, not a numerical clip.\n\nRecord every m8 query's vertex x, independent edge y, reconstructed edge x,\nand vertex differences. m10 and precision m10 full vector logs are complete.\nm12/m14 vector samples are EXACTLY k=0,1,2,3,2^(m-1)-1,2^(m-1),\n2^(m-1)+1,2^(m-1)+2,n, labeled SAMPLED. All scalar logs remain complete\nunless a failure/timeout is explicitly recorded. Count n updates and n+1\nquery-gradient evaluations per complete trajectory, including the terminal\ndiagnostic gradient. Count the independent calibration and precision runs\nseparately. No stored-data comparison is a new trajectory. On timeout the\nlast line gives a committed lower count; at most one update/query is in flight,\nso record that interval rather than inventing an exact completed count.\n\n## Terminal quantities and original benchmark\n\nUse actual F_n to report V_m=4F_n/N. Numerical\nchecks will not promote this text to the fact graph.\n\nFor an integer m>=2 set lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-1/m,\nc=lambda(1+1/m), a=beta/c, r=2/c, w_j=r^j/sum_(k<m)r^k.\nKeep this same enclosing m at every level: H_0=(),\nH_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), n=2^m-1, S=sum H_m. The actual step at\nzero-based index i is h_i=1+a*c^(v2(i+1)), by this recursion.\nHere v2 counts factors of two. For the selected positive zeta put\ndelta=zeta/(1+2S). The initial vector is NOT scaled.\n\nThere is an orthonormal coordinate e_v for each binary word v of length\nless than m. For a leaf s of length m define\nv_s=sum_(j<m)(-1)^(s_(j+1))*sqrt(w_j)*e_(s_1...s_j).\nThe full body K_m is the convex hull of these 2^m vectors, in dimension\n2^m-1. Each leaf has squared norm sum w_j=1. The feature matrix rows\nhave zero sum and are pairwise orthogonal: disjoint subtrees have zero\nproduct; a descendant's two signs cancel on an ancestor's constant\nsign region. Every row is nonzero. Thus the matrix has rank 2^m-1 and\nkernel the constant leaf vector. The leaves are affinely independent,\ntheir average is zero, and zero is interior to K_m. In particular this\nis the CENTERED simplex without a carrier or an extra dimension.\n\nLet K_scaled=delta*K_m and\nF_scaled(x)=max_(g in K_scaled)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2).\nStrict convexity of squared distance gives a unique maximizer g, and\ncompleting the square identifies\ng=P_(delta*K_m)(x)=delta*P_(K_m)(x/delta).\nThe projection characterization is <x-g,v-g><=0 for every vertex v.\nNecessity follows by a derivative along the segment from g to v;\nsufficiency follows by expanding ||x-v||^2-||x-g||^2. Adding the two\nnormal inequalities at x,x' gives ||g-g'||^2<=<g-g',x-x'>, proving\n1-Lipschitz continuity. The maximizing formulas sandwich\nF_scaled(x+d)-F_scaled(x)-<g,d> between zero and\n<P_(delta*K_m)(x+d)-g,d>, bounded by ||d||^2. Hence F_scaled is convex\nand differentiable with the stated gradient and global smoothness L=1.\nInside the body the gradient equals x; an open ball is contained there,\nso no smaller global Lipschitz constant is possible. Every gradient has\nnorm at most delta by convexity and the unit leaf norms. Along t*v_left\nwith t>=delta its norm equals delta, so this cap is sharp.\n\nThe zero gradient is feasible, hence F_scaled>=0 and F_scaled(0)=0.\nFor x!=0 a sufficiently small positive multiple t*x belongs to the\ninterior body and has <x,t*x>-||t*x||^2/2>0. Thus the WHOLE minimum set\nis {0}. With x0=v_left, the unscaled all-left leaf, the true squared\nclosest-minimizer distance is exactly R^2=1. Reported V=2F_scaled/(LR^2)\ntherefore equals 2F_scaled. Neither x0 nor H_m nor L is recalibrated.\nAlthough F_scaled(x)=delta^2 F_K_m(x/delta), its equivalent unit-body\nstart would be v_left/delta, which differs from the old pilot's start.\n\nOff-spine tree automorphisms act by orthogonal signed coordinate\npermutations, preserve K_m, and fix x0. Projection commutes with them\nby uniqueness. A vector fixed by all these automorphisms has zero\noff-spine coordinates, since the child swap at an off-spine node reverses\nthat coordinate. Induction in the actual GD update keeps every x_i in\nthe m-dimensional spine. Group the leaves by first-right depth k<m,\nand retain the all-left leaf as group m. Uniform averaging gives b_k\nwith j coordinate sqrt(w_j) for j<k, -sqrt(w_k) for j=k<m, and zero\nfor j>k; b_m has all coordinates sqrt(w_j). Averaging representations\nproves the fixed section is exactly conv{b_0,...,b_m}. Group masses p_k\nlift uniformly over 2^(m-k-1) leaves for k<m, and p_m is the last mass.\nFor a spine residual, its inner product with every full leaf in a group\nequals the product with b_k.
